# Six Clues – images for the daily mysteries

Generated by `python3 tools/m_images.py` (runs with every export). Lists only the images that are **still missing**.

## How to deliver

- **Format:** JPG, sRGB, quality about 80. Portraits 1024 × 1024 px (under 400 KB). Case covers 1600 × 1200 px, 4:3 (under 500 KB). Evidence photos (`photo-….jpg`) 1024 × 768 px, 4:3 (under 300 KB).
- **Names:** exactly the paths below: `<case>/<person>.jpg`. The site looks for them in `assets/img/m/`.
- **Delivery:** ZIP files whose top level contains the case folders. Partial ZIPs are fine.
- **Order:** the cases at the top are already on the site; daily cases follow in release order.
- **Style:** the same as the Game Night portraits, so the whole site looks like one series.

## Murder at the Masquerade (library – on the site now)

Setting: Theatre producer Isadora Vance has been found dead in her dressing room during the theatre's masked ball.

### `murder-at-the-masquerade/photo-strip-1.jpg`  (evidence photo, 1024 × 768 px, 4:3)
> Candid snapshot as taken with that camera – “STRIP 1” at 22:31, from “Photo booth strip – foyer” in the detective mystery “Murder at the Masquerade”: The Swan pulling a face – wings, bodice and chin in frame, the rest above the top edge. The photo must show exactly this, because players use it as evidence. Style: realistic photograph, slightly faded and grainy like real evidence, natural light for the time of day, same muted noir palette as the rest of the site; fictional people, faces small or turned away, no readable text, numbers or logos.
